Characters: Odile, a woman about 45 with grey-streaked hair and ruddy weathered cheeks, wearing a long faded brick-red wool dress with long sleeves, a thick faded red wool shawl with long fringes draped over her head as a hood and crossed over her chest, an oatmeal linen apron tied at the waist, and worn brown leather shoes; villagers in real, everyday medieval northern fishing-village clothing in many different natural colours, every person dressed differently: layered hand-woven wool tunics, kirtles and hooded cloaks in undyed cream, oatmeal and sheep-grey, walnut brown, faded woad blue, mustard ochre and rust brown, with sheepskin vests, oiled leather jerkins, oilskin capes, wool hoods and caps, leather belts, leg-wraps and worn leather shoes; all colours muted, earthy and weathered, nothing bright, among them the same recurring faces in every scene, always in the same clothes: Jory (about 18, lanky, messy blond hair, grey wool cap, faded woad-blue tunic, sheepskin vest), Ulf (big man in his 30s, shaved head, sheepskin vest over a rust-brown tunic, iron gaff), Sef (wiry man in his 30s, black beard, crooked nose, dark oiled-leather jerkin over a mustard-ochre tunic), Garth (about 60, bald, short grey beard, grey oilskin cape over an oatmeal tunic), Brenna (fishwife in her 40s, red-brown braid, walnut-brown kirtle, cream wool shawl) and Mags (about 70, white hair, sheep-grey kirtle, black wool shawl); everyone else keeps their hood up, faces in shadow; people of different heights and builds. The villagers are busy with their own everyday work, not posing and not watching the camera: fishwives gutting and salting herring at the stalls, people haggling and handing over coins, a man carrying a basket of fish on his shoulder, another rolling a barrel, an old woman mending a net on a stool, children weaving between the stalls, gulls fighting over scraps; people of different heights, facing in many different directions, in natural mid-movement. It is daytime: nobody carries any light. Nobody looks into the camera lens. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects.
